Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_query_parallel(\n    query: str,\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> List[SearchResult]:","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nParallel TF-IDF Implementation\n\nThis module provides parallelized versions of:\n1. TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using process pooling\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup on index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup on batch search with 4 workers\n\nUses a chunk-based approach for distributed work and efficient memory management.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    search_sequential,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Processing Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Intermediate result from a chunk of documents during parallel indexing.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]]\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]]\n    vocabulary: Set[str]\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ChunkSearchResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from searching a chunk of documents.\"\"\"\n\n    scores: List[Tuple[int, float]]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_documents_chunk(\n    documents: List[Document],\n) -> PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute tokenization and TF.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Each worker processes a chunk of documents independently.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: Chunk of documents to process\n\n    Returns:\n        PartialIndexingResult with term frequencies and vocabularies\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return PartialIndexingResult(\n        doc_term_freqs=doc_term_freqs,\n        doc_terms=doc_terms,\n        vocabulary=vocabulary,\n    )\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_terms(\n    args: Tuple[Set[str], Dict[int, Set[str]]],\n) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequencies for a set of terms.\n\n    Worker function for computing document frequencies in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms, all_doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to their document frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, all_doc_terms = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in all_doc_terms if term in all_doc_terms[doc_id])\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _merge_partial_results(\n    partial_results: List[PartialIndexingResult],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, Set[str]], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge partial results from multiple workers.\n\n    Args:\n        partial_results: List of PartialIndexingResult from workers\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    merged_doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    merged_vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for result in partial_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(result.doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(result.doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(result.vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses a chunk-based approach:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n    3. Merge results and compute document frequencies\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Chunk documents and process in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results = pool.map(_process_documents_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 2: Merge partial results\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_partial_results(partial_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 3: Compute document frequencies\n    # Split vocabulary among workers for parallel DF computation\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = [\n        set(vocab_list[i : i + chunk_size])\n        for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    args_list = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms, args_list)\n\n    # Merge document frequencies\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 4: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 5: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 6: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_chunk(\n    args: Tuple[List[int], str, TFIDFIndex, int],\n) -> ChunkSearchResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search a chunk of candidate documents.\n\n    Worker function for parallel search.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, query, index, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        ChunkSearchResult with scores for the documents\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, query, index, top_k = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return ChunkSearchResult(scores=[])\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return ChunkSearchResult(scores=[])\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for candidate documents\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    return ChunkSearchResult(scores=scores)\n\n\ndef _search_query_parallel(\n    query: str,\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> List[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for documents similar to query using parallel processing.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects sorted by relevance\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_tokens:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    if not candidate_docs:\n        return []\n\n    # Convert to sorted list for consistent chunking\n    candidate_list = sorted(list(candidate_docs))\n\n    # Chunk candidates and search in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        candidate_list[i : i + chunk_size]\n        for i in range(0, len(candidate_list), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    args_list = [(chunk, query, index, top_k) for chunk in chunks]\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_search_chunk, args_list)\n\n    # Merge results from all chunks\n    all_scores = []\n    for result in chunk_results:\n        all_scores.extend(result.scores)\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, all_scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Batch Search with Parallel Execution\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: List[Document] = None,\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses process pooling to distribute query processing across workers.\n    Each worker processes one or more queries independently.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results per query, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document title mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Use process pool to search queries in parallel\n    # Create args for each query\n    args_list = [(q, index, top_k) for q in queries]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        # Use starmap to pass multiple arguments\n        results_per_query = pool.starmap(\n            lambda q, idx, k: _search_query_parallel(q, idx, k, num_workers=1),\n            args_list,\n        )\n\n    # Update titles in results\n    for result_list in results_per_query:\n        for result in result_list:\n            result.title = doc_titles.get(result.doc_id, f\"Document {result.doc_id}\")\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_per_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Testing and Benchmarking\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_documents_chunk(\n    documents: List[Document],\n) -> PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute tokenization and TF.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Each worker processes a chunk of documents independently.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: Chunk of documents to process\n\n    Returns:\n        PartialIndexingResult with term frequencies and vocabularies\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return Tuple of (terms, all_doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to their document frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, all_doc_terms = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in all_doc_terms if term in all_doc_terms[doc_id])\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _merge_partial_results(\n    partial_results: ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses a chunk-based approach:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n    3. Merge results and compute document frequencies\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Chunk documents and process in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results = pool.map(_process_documents_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 2: Merge partial results\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_partial_results(partial_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 3: Compute document frequencies\n    # Split vocabulary among workers for parallel DF computation\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = [\n        set(vocab_list[i : i + chunk_size])\n        for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    args_list = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms, args_list)\n\n    # Merge document frequencies\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 4: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 5: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 6: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_chunk(\n    args: Tuple[List[int], str, TFIDFIndex, int],\n) -> ChunkSearchResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search a chunk of candidate documents.\n\n    Worker function for parallel search.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, query, index, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        ChunkSearchResult with scores for the documents\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, query, index, top_k = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return ChunkSearchResult(scores=[])\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return ChunkSearchResult(scores=[])\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for candidate documents\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    return ChunkSearchResult(scores=scores)\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(\n    query: str,\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n) -> List[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using sequential approach within worker.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects sorted by relevance\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_query_parallel(\n    query: str,\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> List[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for documents similar to query using parallel processing.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects sorted by relevance\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_tokens:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    if not candidate_docs:\n        return []\n\n    # Convert to sorted list for consistent chunking\n    candidate_list = sorted(list(candidate_docs))\n\n    # Chunk candidates and search in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        candidate_list[i : i + chunk_size]\n        for i in range(0, len(candidate_list), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    args_list = [(chunk, query, index, top_k) for chunk in chunks]\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_search_chunk, args_list)\n\n    # Merge results from all chunks\n    all_scores = []\n    for result in chunk_results:\n        all_scores.extend(result.scores)\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, all_scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Batch Search with Parallel Execution\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: List[Document] = None,\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses process pooling to distribute query processing across workers.\n    Each worker processes one or more queries independently.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results per query, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document title mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Use process pool to search queries in parallel\n    # Create args for each query\n    args_list = [(q, index, top_k) for q in queries]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        # Use starmap to pass multiple arguments\n        results_per_query = pool.starmap(\n            lambda q, idx, k: _search_query_parallel(q, idx, k, num_workers=1),\n            args_list,\n        )\n\n    # _process_documents_chunk(\n    documents: List[Document],\n) -> PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute tokenization and TF.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Each worker processes a chunk of documents independently.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: Chunk of documents to process\n\n    Returns:\n        PartialIndexingResult with term frequencies and vocabularies\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return PartialIndexingResult(\n        doc_term_freqs=doc_term_freqs,\n        doc_terms=doc_terms,\n        vocabulary=vocabulary,\n    )\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_terms(\n    args: Tuple[Set[str], Dict[int, Set[str]]],\n) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequencies for a set of terms.\n\n    Worker function for computing document frequencies in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms, all_doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to their document frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, all_doc_terms = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in all_doc_terms if term in all_doc_terms[doc_id])\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _merge_partial_results(\n    partial_results: List[PartialIndexingResult],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, Set[str]], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge partial results from multiple workers.\n\n    Args:\n        partial_results: List of PartialIndexingResult from workers\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    merged_doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    merged_vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for result in ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses a chunk-based approach:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n    3. Merge results and compute document frequencies\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Chunk documents and process in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results = pool.map(_process_documents_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 2: Merge partial results\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_partial_results(partial_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 3: Compute document frequencies\n    # Split vocabulary among workers for parallel DF computation\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = [\n        set(vocab_list[i : i + chunk_size])\n        for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    args_list = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms, args_list)\n\n    # Merge document frequencies\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 4: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 5: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 6: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_batch_of_queries(\n    batch_args: Tuple[List[str], TFIDFIndex, int],\n) -> List[List[Tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search a batch of queries sequentially within a worker.\n\n    This worker function processes multiple queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n    Each query is processed independently, returning top-k results as tuples.\n\n    Args:\n        batch_args: Tuple of (query_batch, index, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of score lists, one per query in the batch\n    \"\"\"\n    query_batch, index, top_k = batch_args\n\n    all_results = []\n    for query in query_batch:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        if not candidate_docs:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity for candidates\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n        all_results.append(top_results)\n\n    return all_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Batch Search with Parallel Execution\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: List[Document] = None,\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses process pooling to distribute query batches across workers.\n    Each worker processes a batch of queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results per query, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document title mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Distribute queries into batches (one batch per worker for efficiency)\n    # This reduces pickling overhead by processing multiple queries per worker\n    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = [\n        queries[i : i + queries_per_worker]\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), queries_per_worker)\n    ]\n\n    # Create arguments for each batch\n    batch_args = [(batch, index, top_k) for batch in query_batches]\n\n    # Search batches in parallel\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_batch_of_queries, batch_args)\n\n    # Flatten results: batch_results is List[List[List[Tuple[int, float]]]]\n    # We need List[List[SearchResult]]\n    results_per_query = []\n    for batch_result in batch_results:\n        for score_list in batch_result:\n            result_objects = [\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n                for doc_id, score in score_list\n            ]\n            results_per_query.append(result_objects)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_per_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Testing and Benchmarking\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # ParallelIndexingResult:\n   157→    \"\"\"\n   158→    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n   159→\n   160→    Uses a chunk-based approach:\n   161→    1. Split documents into chunks\n   162→    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n   163→    3. Merge results and compute document frequencies\n   164→    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n   165→    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n   166→\n   167→    Args:\n   168→        documents: List of Document objects to index\n   169→        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n   170→        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n   171→\n   172→    Returns:\n   173→        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n   174→    \"\"\"\n   175→    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n   176→\n   177→    if num_workers is None:\n   178→        num_workers = cpu_count()\n   179→\n   180→    index = TFIDFIndex()\n   181→    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n   182→\n   183→    # Tokenize query\n   275→        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n   276→        if not query_tokens:\n   277→            all_results.append([])\n   278→            continue\n   279→\n   280→        # Compute query TF\n   281→        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n   282→\n   283→        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n   284→        query_vector = {}\n   285→        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n   286→        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n   287→            if term in index.idf:\n   288→                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n   289→                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n   290→                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n   291→\n   292→        if not query_vector:\n   293→            all_results.append([])\n   294→            continue\n   295→\n   296→        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n   297→\n   298→        # Find candidate documents\n   299→        candidate_docs = set()\n   300→        for term in query_vector:\n   301→            if term in index.inverted_index:\n   302→                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n   303→                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n   304→\n   305→        if not candidate_docs:\n   306→            all_results.append([])\n   307→            continue\n   308→\n   309→        # Compute cosine similarity for candidates\n   310→        scores = []\n   311→        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n   312→            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n   313→            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n   314→\n   315→            if doc_norm == 0:\n   316→                continue\n   317→\n   318→            # Dot product\n   319→            dot_product = sum(\n   320→                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n   321→                for term in query_vector\n   322→            )\n   323→\n   324→            # Cosine similarity\n   325→            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n   326→            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n   327→\n   328→        # Get top-k results\n   329→        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n   330→        all_results.append(top_results)\n   331→\n   332→    return all_results\n   333→\n   334→\n   335→# ============================================================================\n   336→# Batch Search with Parallel Execution\n   337→# ============================================================================\n   338→\n   339→\n   340→def batch_search_parallel(\n   341→    queries: List[str],\n   342→    index: TFIDFIndex,\n   343→    top_k: int = 10,\n   344→    num_workers: int = None,\n   345→    documents: List[Document] = None,\n   346→) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n   347→    \"\"\"\n   348→    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n   349→\n   350→    Uses process pooling to distribute query batches across workers.\n   351→    Each worker processes a batch of queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n   352→\n   353→    Args:\n   354→        queries: List of search query strings\n   355→        index: TF-IDF index to search\n   356→        top_k: Number of top results per query\n   357→        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n   358→        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n   359→\n   360→    Returns:\n   361→        Tuple of (results per query, elapsed_time)\n   362→    \"\"\"\n   363→    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n   364→\n   365→    if num_workers is None:\n   366→        num_workers = cpu_count()\n   367→\n   368→    # Build document title mapping\n   369→    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n   370→\n   371→    # Distribute queries into batches (one batch per worker for efficiency)\n   372→    # This reduces pickling overhead by processing multiple queries per worker\n   373→    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers + 1)\n   374→    query_batches = [\n   375→        queries[i : i + queries_per_worker]\n   376→        for i in range(0, len(queries), queries_per_worker)\n   377→    ]\n   378→\n   379→    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using process pooling\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup on index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup on batch search with 4 workers\n\nUses a chunk-based approach for distributed work and efficient memory management.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    search_sequential,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Processing Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Intermediate result from a chunk of documents during parallel indexing.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]]\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]]\n    vocabulary: Set[str]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_documents_chunk(\n    documents: List[Document],\n) -> PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute tokenization and TF.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Each worker processes a chunk of documents independently.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: Chunk of documents to process\n\n    Returns:\n        PartialIndexingResult with term frequencies and vocabularies\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return PartialIndexingResult(\n        doc_term_freqs=doc_term_freqs,\n        doc_terms=doc_terms,\n        vocabulary=vocabulary,\n    )\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_terms(\n    args: Tuple[Set[str], Dict[int, Set[str]]],\n) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequencies for a set of terms.\n\n    Worker function for computing document frequencies in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms, all_doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to their document frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, all_doc_terms = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in all_doc_terms if term in all_doc_terms[doc_id])\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _merge_partial_results(\n    partial_results: List[PartialIndexingResult],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, Set[str]], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge partial results from multiple workers.\n\n    Args:\n        partial_results: List of PartialIndexingResult from workers\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    merged_doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    merged_vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for result in partial_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(result.doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(result.doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(result.vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses a chunk-based approach:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n    3. Merge results and compute document frequencies\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Chunk documents and process in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results = pool.map(_process_documents_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 2: Merge partial results\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_partial_results(partial_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 3: Compute document frequencies\n    # Split vocabulary among workers for parallel DF computation\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = [\n        set(vocab_list[i : i + chunk_size])\n        for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    args_list = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms, args_list)\n\n    # Merge document frequencies\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 4: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 5: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 6: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_batch_of_queries(\n    batch_args: Tuple[List[str], TFIDFIndex, int],\n) -> List[List[Tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search a batch of queries sequentially within a worker.\n\n    This worker function processes multiple queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n    Each query is processed independently, returning top-k results as tuples.\n\n    Args:\n        batch_args: Tuple of (query_batch, index, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of score lists, one per query in the batch\n    \"\"\"\n    query_batch, index, top_k = batch_args\n\n    all_results = []\n    for query in query_batch:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        if not candidate_docs:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity for candidates\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n        all_results.append(top_results)\n\n    return all_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Batch Search with Parallel Execution\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: List[Document] = None,\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses process pooling to distribute query batches across workers.\n    Each worker processes a batch of queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results per query, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document title mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Distribute queries into batches (one batch per worker for efficiency)\n    # This reduces pickling overhead by processing multiple queries per worker\n    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = [\n        queries[i : i + queries_per_worker]\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), queries_per_worker)\n    ]\n\n    # Create arguments for each batch\n    batch_args = [(batch, index, top_k) for batch in query_batches]\n\n    # Search batches in parallel\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_batch_of_queries, batch_args)\n\n    # Flatten results: batch_results is List[List[List[Tuple[int, float]]]]\n    # We need List[List[SearchResult]]\n    results_per_query = []\n    for batch_result in batch_results:\n        for score_list in batch_result:\n            result_objects = [\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n                for doc_id, score in score_list\n            ]\n            results_per_query.append(result_objects)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_per_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Testing and Benchmarking\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Generate test corpus\n    num_docs = 5000\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(num_docs, seed=42)\n    print(f\"Generated {len(documents)} documents\")\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using process pooling\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup on index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup on batch search with 4 workers\n\nUses a chunk-based approach for distributed work and efficient memory management.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    search_sequential,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Processing Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Intermediate result from a chunk of documents during parallel indexing.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]]\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]]\n    vocabulary: Set[str]\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ChunkSearchResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from searching a chunk of documents.\"\"\"\n\n    scores: List[Tuple[int, float]]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_documents_chunk(\n    documents: List[Document],\n) -> PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute tokenization and TF.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Each worker processes a chunk of documents independently.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: Chunk of documents to process\n\n    Returns:\n        PartialIndexingResult with term frequencies and vocabularies\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return PartialIndexingResult(\n        doc_term_freqs=doc_term_freqs,\n        doc_terms=doc_terms,\n        vocabulary=vocabulary,\n    )\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_terms(\n    args: Tuple[Set[str], Dict[int, Set[str]]],\n) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequencies for a set of terms.\n\n    Worker function for computing document frequencies in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms, all_doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to their document frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, all_doc_terms = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in all_doc_terms if term in all_doc_terms[doc_id])\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _merge_partial_results(\n    partial_results: List[PartialIndexingResult],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, Set[str]], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge partial results from multiple workers.\n\n    Args:\n        partial_results: List of PartialIndexingResult from workers\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    merged_doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    merged_vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for result in partial_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(result.doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(result.doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(result.vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses a chunk-based approach:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n    3. Merge results and compute document frequencies\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Chunk documents and process in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results = pool.map(_process_documents_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 2: Merge partial results\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_partial_results(partial_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 3: Compute document frequencies\n    # Split vocabulary among workers for parallel DF computation\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = [\n        set(vocab_list[i : i + chunk_size])\n        for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    args_list = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms, args_list)\n\n    # Merge document frequencies\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 4: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 5: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 6: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_chunk(\n    args: Tuple[List[int], str, TFIDFIndex, int],\n) -> ChunkSearchResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search a chunk of candidate documents.\n\n    Worker function for parallel search.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, query, index, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        ChunkSearchResult with scores for the documents\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, query, index, top_k = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return ChunkSearchResult(scores=[])\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return ChunkSearchResult(scores=[])\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for candidate documents\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    return ChunkSearchResult(scores=scores)\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(\n    query: str,\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n) -> List[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query using sequential approach within worker.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects sorted by relevance\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_query_parallel(\n    query: str,\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> List[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for documents similar to query using parallel processing.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results to return\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects sorted by relevance\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_tokens:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    if not candidate_docs:\n        return []\n\n    # Convert to sorted list for consistent chunking\n    candidate_list = sorted(list(candidate_docs))\n\n    # Chunk candidates and search in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        candidate_list[i : i + chunk_size]\n        for i in range(0, len(candidate_list), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    args_list = [(chunk, query, index, top_k) for chunk in chunks]\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        chunk_results = pool.map(_search_chunk, args_list)\n\n    # Merge results from all chunks\n    all_scores = []\n    for result in chunk_results:\n        all_scores.extend(result.scores)\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, all_scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(\n            SearchResult(\n                doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=f\"Document {doc_id}\"\n            )\n        )\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Batch Search with Parallel Execution\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: List[Document] = None,\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses process pooling to distribute query processing across workers.\n    Each worker processes one or more queries independently.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results per query, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document title mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Use process pool to search queries in parallel\n    # Create args for each query - use tuple format for map compatibility\n    args_list = [(q, index, top_k) for q in queries]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        # Use starmap to unpack tuple arguments to _search_single_query\n        results_per_query = pool.starmap(_search_single_query, args_list)\n\n    # Update titles in results\n    for result_list in results_per_query:\n        for result in result_list:\n            result.title = doc_titles.get(result.doc_id, f\"Document {result.doc_id}\")\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_per_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Testing and Benchmarking\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Generate test corpus\n    num_docs = 5000\n    print(f\"\\nGenerating {num_docs} documents...\")\n    documents = generate_corpus(num_docs, seed=42)\n    print(f\"Generated {len(documents)} documents\")\n\n    # Test correctness: Build index both ways\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"-\" * 70)\n    print(\"CORRECTNESS TEST: Building indexes sequentially and in parallel\")\n    print(\"-\" * 70)\n\n    print(\"\\nBuilding sequential index...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(\n        f\"Sequential: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s, \"\n        f\"vocab={seq_result.vocabulary_size}, docs={seq_result.num_documents}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\"Building parallel index (4 workers)...\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=4, chunk_size=500)\n    print(\n        f\"Parallel:   {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f}s, \"\n        f\"vocab={par_result.vocabulary_size}, docs={par_result.num_documents}\"\n    )\n\n    # Verify results match\n    assert seq_result.vocabulary_size == par_result.vocabulary_size, (\n        f\"Vocabulary size mismatch: {seq_result.vocabulary_size} vs \"\n        f\"{par_result.vocabulary_size}\"\n    )\n    assert seq_result.num_documents == par_result.num_documents, (\n        f\"Document count mismatch: {seq_result.num_documents} vs \"\n        f\"{par_result.num_documents}\"\n    )\n\n    print(\"\\n✓ Index structure verification passed\")\n\n    #
