{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/python-parallelization\n\n# Python Parallelization Skill\n\nTransform sequential Python code to leverage parallel and concurrent execution patterns.\n\n## Workflow\n\n1. **Analyze** the code to identify parallelization candidates\n2. **Classify** the workload type (CPU-bound, I/O-bound, or data-parallel)\n3. **Select** the appropriate parallelization strategy\n4. **Transform** the code with proper synchronization and error handling\n5. **Verify** correctness and measure expected speedup\n\n## Parallelization Decision Tree\n\n```\nIs the bottleneck CPU-bound or I/O-bound?\n\nCPU-bound (computation-heavy):\n├── Independent iterations? → multiprocessing.Pool / ProcessPoolExecutor\n├── Shared state needed? → multiprocessing with Manager or shared memory\n├── NumPy/Pandas operations? → Vectorization first, then consider numba/dask\n└── Large data chunks? → chunked processing with Pool.map\n\nI/O-bound (network, disk, database):\n├── Many independent requests? → asyncio with aiohttp/aiofiles\n├── Legacy sync code? → ThreadPoolExecutor\n├── Mixed sync/async? → asyncio.to_thread()\n└── Database queries? → Connection pooling + async drivers\n\nData-parallel (array/matrix ops):\n├── NumPy arrays? → Vectorize, avoid Python loops\n├── Pandas DataFrames? → Use built-in vectorized methods\n├── Large datasets? → Dask for out-of-core parallelism\n└── GPU available? → Consider CuPy or JAX\n```\n\n## Transformation Patterns\n\n### Pattern 1: Loop to ProcessPoolExecutor (CPU-bound)\n\n**Before:**\n```python\nresults = []\nfor item in items:\n    results.append(expensive_computation(item))\n```\n\n**After:**\n```python\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor\n\nwith ProcessPoolExecutor() as executor:\n    results = list(executor.map(expensive_computation, items))\n```\n\n### Pattern 2: Sequential I/O to Async (I/O-bound)\n\n**Before:**\n```python\nimport requests\n\ndef fetch_all(urls):\n    return [requests.get(url).json() for url in urls]\n```\n\n**After:**\n```python\nimport asyncio\nimport aiohttp\n\nasync def fetch_all(urls):\n    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:\n        tasks = [fetch_one(session, url) for url in urls]\n        return await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n\nasync def fetch_one(session, url):\n    async with session.get(url) as response:\n        return await response.json()\n```\n\n### Pattern 3: Nested Loops to Vectorization\n\n**Before:**\n```python\nresult = []\nfor i in range(len(a)):\n    row = []\n    for j in range(len(b)):\n        row.append(a[i] * b[j])\n    result.append(row)\n```\n\n**After:**\n```python\nimport numpy as np\nresult = np.outer(a, b)\n```\n\n### Pattern 4: Mixed CPU/IO with asyncio\n\n```python\nimport asyncio\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor\n\nasync def hybrid_pipeline(data, urls):\n    loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()\n\n    # CPU-bound in process pool\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor() as pool:\n        processed = await loop.run_in_executor(pool, cpu_heavy_fn, data)\n\n    # I/O-bound with async\n    results = await asyncio.gather(*[fetch(url) for url in urls])\n\n    return processed, results\n```\n\n## Parallelization Candidates\n\nLook for these patterns in code:\n\n| Pattern | Indicator | Strategy |\n|---------|-----------|----------|\n| `for item in collection` with independent iterations | No shared mutation | `Pool.map` / `executor.map` |\n| Multiple `requests.get()` or file reads | Sequential I/O | `asyncio.gather()` |\n| Nested loops over arrays | Numerical computation | NumPy vectorization |\n| `time.sleep()` or blocking waits | Waiting on external | Threading or async |\n| Large list comprehensions | Independent transforms | `Pool.map` with chunking |\n\n## Safety Requirements\n\nAlways preserve correctness when parallelizing:\n\n1. **Identify shared state** - variables modified across iterations break parallelism\n2. **Check dependencies** - iteration N depending on N-1 requires sequential execution\n3. **Handle exceptions** - wrap parallel code in try/except, use `executor.submit()` for granular error handling\n4. **Manage resources** - use context managers, limit worker count to avoid exhaustion\n5. **Preserve ordering** - use `map()` over `submit()` when order matters\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n- **GIL trap**: Threading doesn't help CPU-bound Python code—use multiprocessing\n- **Pickle failures**: Lambda functions and nested classes can't be pickled for multiprocessing\n- **Memory explosion**: ProcessPoolExecutor copies data to each process—use shared memory for large data\n- **Async in sync**: Can't just add `async` to existing code—requires restructuring call chain\n- **Over-parallelization**: Parallel overhead exceeds gains for small workloads (<1000 items typically)\n\n## Verification Checklist\n\nBefore finalizing transformed code:\n\n- [ ] Output matches sequential version for test inputs\n- [ ] No race conditions (shared mutable state properly synchronized)\n- [ ] Exceptions are caught and handled appropriately\n- [ ] Resources are properly cleaned up (pools closed, connections released)\n- [ ] Worker count is bounded (default or explicit limit)\n- [ ] Added appropriate imports\n\n\nARGUMENTS: Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n    3. Merge results and compute document frequencies\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Chunk documents and process in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results = pool.map(_process_documents_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 2: Merge partial results\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_partial_results(partial_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 3: Compute document frequencies\n    # Split vocabulary among workers for parallel DF computation\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = [\n        set(vocab_list[i : i + chunk_size])\n        for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    args_list = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms, args_list)\n\n    # Merge document frequencies\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 4: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 5: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 6: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helpers","numLines":100,"startLine":152,"totalLines":543}}}

TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using process pooling\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup on index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup on batch search with 4 workers\n\nUses a chunk-based approach for distributed work and efficient memory management.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import ============================================================================\n# Parallel Processing Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_documents_chunk(\n    documents: List[Document],\n) -> PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute tokenization and TF.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Each worker processes a chunk of documents independently.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: Chunk of documents to process\n\n    Returns:\n        PartialIndexingResult with term frequencies and vocabularies\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return PartialIndexingResult(\n        doc_term_freqs=doc_term_freqs,\n        doc_terms=doc_terms,\n        vocabulary=vocabulary,\n    )\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_terms(\n    args: Tuple[Set[str], Dict[int, Set[str]]],\n) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document frequencies for a set of terms.\n\n    Worker function for computing document frequencies in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms, all_doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping terms to their document frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    terms, all_doc_terms = args\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in all_doc_terms if term in all_doc_terms[doc_id])\n        result[term] = df\n    return result\n\n\ndef _merge_partial_results(\n    partial_results: List[PartialIndexingResult],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, Set[str]], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge partial results from multiple workers.\n\n    Args:\n        partial_results: List of PartialIndexingResult from workers\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    merged_doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    merged_vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for result in partial_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(result.doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(result.doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(result.vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses a chunk-based approach:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n    3. Merge results and compute document frequencies\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Chunk documents and process in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results = pool.map(_process_documents_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 2: Merge partial results\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_partial_results(partial_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 3: Compute document frequencies\n    # Split vocabulary among workers for parallel DF computation\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = [\n        set(vocab_list[i : i + chunk_size])\n        for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    args_list = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms, args_list)\n\n    # Merge document frequencies\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 4: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 5: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 6: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_batch_of_queries(\n    batch_args: Tuple[List[str], TFIDFIndex, int],\n) -> List[List[Tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search a batch of queries sequentially within a worker.\n\n    This worker function processes multiple queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n    Each query is processed independently, returning top-k results as tuples.\n\n    Args:\n        batch_args: Tuple of (query_batch, index, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of score lists, one per query in the batch\n    \"\"\"\n    query_batch, index, top_k = batch_args\n\n    all_results = []\n    for query in query_batch:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        if not candidate_docs:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity for candidates\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n        all_results.append(top_results)\n\n    return all_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Batch Search with Parallel Execution\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: List[Document] = None,\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses process pooling to distribute query batches across workers.\n    Each worker processes a batch of queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results per query, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document title mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Distribute queries into batches (one batch per worker for efficiency)\n    # This reduces pickling overhead by processing multiple queries per worker\n    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = [\n        queries[i : i + queries_per_worker]\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), queries_per_worker)\n    ]\n\n    # Create arguments for each batch\n    batch_args = [(batch, index, top_k) for batch in query_batches]\n\n    # Search batches in parallel\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_batch_of_queries, batch_args)\n\n    # Flatten results: batch_results is List[List[List[Tuple[int, float]]]]\n    # We need List[List[SearchResult]]\n    results_per_query = []\n    for batch_result in batch_results:\n        for score_list in batch_result:\n            result_objects = [\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n                for doc_id, score in score_list\n            ]\n            results_per_query.append(result_objects)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_per_query, elapsed\n\n\n# Step 6: Merge parallel results\n    for inverted_index_chunk, doc_vectors_chunk, doc_norms_chunk in results:\n        index.inverted_index.update(inverted_index_chunk)\n\n        # Merge document vectors (accumulate across chunks)\n        for doc_id, doc_vector_chunk in doc_vectors_chunk.items():\n            if doc_id not in index.doc_vectors:\n                index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = {}\n            index.doc_vectors[doc_id].update(doc_vector_chunk)\n\n        # Norms need to be recomputed after all chunks are merged\n        for doc_id, norm in doc_norms_chunk.items():\n            if doc_id not in index.doc_norms:\n                index.doc_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    # Recompute norms properly after merging all chunks\n    for doc_id, doc_vector in index.doc_vectors.items():\n        norm_squared = sum(v * v for v in doc_vector.values())\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nParallel TF-IDF Implementation\n\nThis module provides parallelized versions of:\n1. TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using process pooling\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup on index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup on batch search with 4 workers\n\nUses a chunk-based approach for distributed work and efficient memory management.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    search_sequential,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Processing Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Intermediate result from a chunk of documents during parallel indexing.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]]\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]]\n    vocabulary: Set[str]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_documents_chunk(\n    documents: List[Document],\n) -> PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute tokenization and TF.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Each worker processes a chunk of documents independently.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: Chunk of documents to process\n\n    Returns:\n        PartialIndexingResult with term frequencies and vocabularies\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return PartialIndexingResult(\n        doc_term_freqs=doc_term_freqs,\n        doc_terms=doc_terms,\n        vocabulary=vocabulary,\n    )\n\n\ndef _compute_posting_lists_and_norms(\n    args: Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[str, float], Set[str]],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[str, list], Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute posting lists, document vectors, and norms for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This moves more work to the parallel phase to reduce overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, idf_dict, terms_chunk)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (inverted_index_chunk, doc_vectors_chunk, doc_norms_chunk)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs, idf_dict, terms_chunk = args\n\n    inverted_index_chunk = {}\n    doc_vectors_chunk = {}\n    doc_norms_chunk = {}\n\n    # Build posting lists and doc vectors for each term in this chunk\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        if term not in idf_dict:\n            continue\n\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        if posting_list:\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            inverted_index_chunk[term] = posting_list\n\n        # Update document vectors\n        for doc_id, tf in [(doc_id, tf_dict[term]) for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items() if term in tf_dict]:\n            if doc_id not in doc_vectors_chunk:\n                doc_vectors_chunk[doc_id] = {}\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vectors_chunk[doc_id][term] = tfidf\n\n    # Compute norms from accumulated vectors\n    for doc_id, doc_vector in doc_vectors_chunk.items():\n        norm_squared = sum(v * v for v in doc_vector.values())\n        doc_norms_chunk[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return inverted_index_chunk, doc_vectors_chunk, doc_norms_chunk\n\n\ndef _merge_partial_results(\n    partial_results: List[PartialIndexingResult],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, Set[str]], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge partial results from multiple workers.\n\n    Args:\n        partial_results: List of PartialIndexingResult from workers\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    merged_doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    merged_vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for result in partial_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(result.doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(result.doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(result.vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses a chunk-based approach:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n    3. Merge results and compute document frequencies\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Chunk documents and process in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results = pool.map(_process_documents_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 2: Merge partial results\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_partial_results(partial_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 3: Compute document frequencies\n    # Split vocabulary among workers for parallel DF computation\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = [\n        set(vocab_list[i : i + chunk_size])\n        for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    args_list = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms, args_list)\n\n    # Merge document frequencies\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 4: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 5: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 6: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_batch_of_queries(\n    batch_args: Tuple[List[str], TFIDFIndex, int],\n) -> List[List[Tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search a batch of queries sequentially within a worker.\n\n    This worker function processes multiple queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n    Each query is processed independently, returning top-k results as tuples.\n\n    Args:\n        batch_args: Tuple of (query_batch, index, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of score lists, one per query in the batch\n    \"\"\"\n    query_batch, index, top_k = batch_args\n\n    all_results = []\n    for query in query_batch:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        if not candidate_docs:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity for candidates\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n        all_results.append(top_results)\n\n    return all_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Batch Search with Parallel Execution\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: List[Document] = None,\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses process pooling to distribute query batches across workers.\n    Each worker processes a batch of queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results per query, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document title mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Distribute queries into batches (one batch per worker for efficiency)\n    # This reduces pickling overhead by processing multiple queries per worker\n    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = [\n        queries[i : i + queries_per_worker]\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), queries_per_worker)\n    ]\n\n    # Create arguments for each batch\n    batch_args = [(batch, index, top_k) for batch in query_batches]\n\n    # Search batches in parallel\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_batch_of_queries, batch_args)\n\n    # Flatten results: batch_results is List[List[List[Tuple[int, float]]]]\n    # We need List[List[SearchResult]]\n    results_per_query = []\n    for batch_result in batch_results:\n        for score_list in batch_result:\n            result_objects = [\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n                for doc_id, score in score_list\n            ]\n            results_per_query.append(result_objects)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_per_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Testing and Benchmarking\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using process pooling\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup on index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup on batch search with 4 workers\n\nUses a chunk-based approach for distributed work and efficient memory management.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom multiprocessing import Pool, cpu_count\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    STOP_WORDS,\n    TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    search_sequential,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Processing Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Intermediate result from a chunk of documents during parallel indexing.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]]\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]]\n    vocabulary: Set[str]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_documents_chunk(\n    documents: List[Document],\n) -> PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute tokenization and TF.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Each worker processes a chunk of documents independently.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: Chunk of documents to process\n\n    Returns:\n        PartialIndexingResult with term frequencies and vocabularies\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return PartialIndexingResult(\n        doc_term_freqs=doc_term_freqs,\n        doc_terms=doc_terms,\n        vocabulary=vocabulary,\n    )\n\n\ndef _compute_posting_lists_and_norms(\n    args: Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[str, float], Set[str]],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[str, list], Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute posting lists, document vectors, and norms for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This moves more work to the parallel phase to reduce overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, idf_dict, terms_chunk)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (inverted_index_chunk, doc_vectors_chunk, doc_norms_chunk)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs, idf_dict, terms_chunk = args\n\n    inverted_index_chunk = {}\n    doc_vectors_chunk = {}\n    doc_norms_chunk = {}\n\n    # Build posting lists and doc vectors for each term in this chunk\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        if term not in idf_dict:\n            continue\n\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        if posting_list:\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            inverted_index_chunk[term] = posting_list\n\n        # Update document vectors\n        for doc_id, tf in [(doc_id, tf_dict[term]) for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items() if term in tf_dict]:\n            if doc_id not in doc_vectors_chunk:\n                doc_vectors_chunk[doc_id] = {}\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vectors_chunk[doc_id][term] = tfidf\n\n    # Compute norms from accumulated vectors\n    for doc_id, doc_vector in doc_vectors_chunk.items():\n        norm_squared = sum(v * v for v in doc_vector.values())\n        doc_norms_chunk[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return inverted_index_chunk, doc_vectors_chunk, doc_norms_chunk\n\n\ndef _merge_partial_results(\n    partial_results: List[PartialIndexingResult],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, Set[str]], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge partial results from multiple workers.\n\n    Args:\n        partial_results: List of PartialIndexingResult from workers\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    merged_doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    merged_vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for result in partial_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(result.doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(result.doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(result.vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses a chunk-based approach:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n    3. Compute document frequencies sequentially (fast)\n    4. Compute IDF scores\n    5. Parallelize building inverted index and document vectors\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Chunk documents and process in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results = pool.map(_process_documents_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 2: Merge partial results\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_partial_results(partial_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 3: Compute document frequencies (fast enough sequentially)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 4: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 5: Parallelize building posting lists and document vectors\n    # Split vocabulary into chunks for parallel processing\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = [\n        set(vocab_list[i : i + chunk_size])\n        for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    # Create args for each chunk: (doc_term_freqs, idf_dict, terms_chunk)\n    args_list = [(doc_term_freqs, index.idf, chunk) for chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_compute_posting_lists_and_norms, args_list)\n\n    # Step 6: Merge parallel results\n    for inverted_index_chunk, doc_vectors_chunk, doc_norms_chunk in results:\n        index.inverted_index.update(inverted_index_chunk)\n\n        # Merge document vectors (accumulate across chunks)\n        for doc_id, doc_vector_chunk in doc_vectors_chunk.items():\n            if doc_id not in index.doc_vectors:\n                index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = {}\n            index.doc_vectors[doc_id].update(doc_vector_chunk)\n\n        # Norms need to be recomputed after all chunks are merged\n        for doc_id, norm in doc_norms_chunk.items():\n            if doc_id not in index.doc_norms:\n                index.doc_norms[doc_id] = norm\n\n    # Recompute norms properly after merging all chunks\n    for doc_id, doc_vector in index.doc_vectors.items():\n        norm_squared = sum(v * v for v in doc_vector.values())\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_batch_of_queries(\n    batch_args: Tuple[List[str], TFIDFIndex, int],\n) -> List[List[Tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search a batch of queries sequentially within a worker.\n\n    This worker function processes multiple queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n    Each query is processed independently, returning top-k results as tuples.\n\n    Args:\n        batch_args: Tuple of (query_batch, index, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of score lists, one per query in the batch\n    \"\"\"\n    query_batch, index, top_k = batch_args\n\n    all_results = []\n    for query in query_batch:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        if not candidate_docs:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity for candidates\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n        all_results.append(top_results)\n\n    return all_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Batch Search with Parallel Execution\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: List[Document] = None,\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses process pooling to distribute query batches across workers.\n    Each worker processes a batch of queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results per query, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document title mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Distribute queries into batches (one batch per worker for efficiency)\n    # This reduces pickling overhead by processing multiple queries per worker\n    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = [\n        queries[i : i + queries_per_worker]\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), queries_per_worker)\n    ]\n\n    # Create arguments for each batch\n    batch_args = [(batch, index, top_k) for batch in query_batches]\n\n    # Search batches in parallel\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_batch_of_queries, batch_args)\n\n    # Flatten results: batch_results is List[List[List[Tuple[int, float]]]]\n    # We need List[List[SearchResult]]\n    results_per_query = []\n    for batch_result in batch_results:\n        for score_list in batch_result:\n            result_objects = [\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n                for doc_id, score in score_list\n            ]\n            results_per_query.append(result_objects)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_per_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Testing and Benchmarking\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # TOKEN_PATTERN,\n    IndexingResult,\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    search_sequential,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Intermediate result from a chunk of documents during parallel indexing.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]]\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]]\n    vocabulary: Set[str]\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_documents_chunk(\n    documents: List[Document],\n) -> PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute tokenization and TF.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Each worker processes a chunk of documents independently.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: Chunk of documents to process\n\n    Returns:\n        PartialIndexingResult with term frequencies and vocabularies\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return PartialIndexingResult(\n        doc_term_freqs=doc_term_freqs,\n        doc_terms=doc_terms,\n        vocabulary=vocabulary,\n    )\n\n\ndef _compute_posting_lists_and_norms(\n    args: Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[str, float], Set[str]],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[str, list], Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute posting lists, document vectors, and norms for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This moves more work to the parallel phase to reduce overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, idf_dict, terms_chunk)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (inverted_index_chunk, doc_vectors_chunk, doc_norms_chunk)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs, idf_dict, terms_chunk = args\n\n    inverted_index_chunk = {}\n    doc_vectors_chunk = {}\n    doc_norms_chunk = {}\n\n    # Build posting lists and doc vectors for each term in this chunk\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        if term not in idf_dict:\n            continue\n\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        if posting_list:\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            inverted_index_chunk[term] = posting_list\n\n        # Update document vectors\n        for doc_id, tf in [(doc_id, tf_dict[term]) for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items() if term in tf_dict]:\n            if doc_id not in doc_vectors_chunk:\n                doc_vectors_chunk[doc_id] = {}\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vectors_chunk[doc_id][term] = tfidf\n\n    # Compute norms from accumulated vectors\n    for doc_id, doc_vector in doc_vectors_chunk.items():\n        norm_squared = sum(v * v for v in doc_vector.values())\n        doc_norms_chunk[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return inverted_index_chunk, doc_vectors_chunk, doc_norms_chunk\n\n\ndef _merge_partial_results(\n    partial_results: List[PartialIndexingResult],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, Set[str]], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge partial results from multiple workers.\n\n    Args:\n        partial_results: List of PartialIndexingResult from workers\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    merged_doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    merged_vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for result in partial_results:\n        merged_doc_term_freqs.update(result.doc_term_freqs)\n        merged_doc_terms.update(result.doc_terms)\n        merged_vocabulary.update(result.vocabulary)\n\n    return merged_doc_term_freqs, merged_doc_terms, merged_vocabulary\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: List[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Uses a chunk-based approach:\n    1. Split documents into chunks\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n    3. Compute document frequencies sequentially (fast)\n    4. Compute IDF scores\n    5. Parallelize building inverted index and document vectors\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Chunk documents and process in parallel\n    chunks = [\n        documents[i : i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results = pool.map(_process_documents_chunk, chunks)\n\n    # Step 2: Merge partial results\n    doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary = _merge_partial_results(partial_results)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 3: Compute document frequencies (fast enough sequentially)\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 4: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 5: Build inverted index and document vectors (sequential is fastest for this data)\n    # The vocabulary is small (~400 terms), so parallel overhead exceeds gains.\n    # Focus parallelism on document processing instead.\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 6: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helpers\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_batch_of_queries(\n    batch_args: Tuple[List[str], TFIDFIndex, int],\n) -> List[List[Tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search a batch of queries sequentially within a worker.\n\n    This worker function processes multiple queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n    Each query is processed independently, returning top-k results as tuples.\n\n    Args:\n        batch_args: Tuple of (query_batch, index, top_k)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of score lists, one per query in the batch\n    \"\"\"\n    query_batch, index, top_k = batch_args\n\n    all_results = []\n    for query in query_batch:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        if not candidate_docs:\n            all_results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity for candidates\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n        all_results.append(top_results)\n\n    return all_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Batch Search with Parallel Execution\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: List[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: List[Document] = None,\n) -> Tuple[List[List[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses process pooling to distribute query batches across workers.\n    Each worker processes a batch of queries to reduce pickling overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results per query, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Build document title mapping\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Distribute queries into batches (one batch per worker for efficiency)\n    # This reduces pickling overhead by processing multiple queries per worker\n    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = [\n        queries[i : i + queries_per_worker]\n        for i in range(0, len(queries), queries_per_worker)\n    ]\n\n    # Create arguments for each batch\n    batch_args = [(batch, index, top_k) for batch in query_batches]\n\n    # Search batches in parallel\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_batch_of_queries, batch_args)\n\n    # Flatten results: batch_results is List[List[List[Tuple[int, float]]]]\n    # We need List[List[SearchResult]]\n    results_per_query = []\n    for batch_result in batch_results:\n        for score_list in batch_result:\n            result_objects = [\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n                for doc_id, score in score_list\n            ]\n            results_per_query.append(result_objects)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results_per_query, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Testing and Benchmarking\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Parallelize building inverted index and document vectors\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Split documents into chunks (sized for optimal parallelism)\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n    3. Merge results and compute document frequencies\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. Compute document vector norms for cosine similarity\n\n    The key optimization is adjusting chunk_size for the document count to ensure\n    good work distribution across processors without excessive overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500, auto-adjusted)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Auto-adjust chunk size for better parallelism efficiency\n    # Target: ~2-3 chunks per worker for good load balancing\n    target_chunks = num_workers * 2.5\n    auto_chunk_size = max(200, int(len(documents) / target_chunks))\n\n    # Split documents into chunks (sized for optimal parallelism)\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel (tokenization + TF computation)\n    3. Merge results and compute document frequencies\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    5. TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch similarity search using process pooling\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup on index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup on batch search with 4 workers\n\nUses a chunk-based approach for distributed work and efficient memory management.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import ============================================================================\n# Parallel Processing Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: _process_documents_chunk(\n    documents: List[Document],\n) -> PartialIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a chunk of documents to compute tokenization and TF.\n\n    This is the worker function for parallel document processing.\n    Each worker processes a chunk of documents independently.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: Chunk of documents to process\n\n    Returns:\n        PartialIndexingResult with term frequencies and vocabularies\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str, float]] = {}\n    doc_terms: Dict[int, Set[str]] = {}\n    vocabulary: Set[str] = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return PartialIndexingResult(\n        doc_term_freqs=doc_term_freqs,\n        doc_terms=doc_terms,\n        vocabulary=vocabulary,\n    )\n\n\ndef _compute_posting_lists_and_norms(\n    args: Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[str, float], Set[str]],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[str, list], Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute posting lists, document vectors, and norms for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This moves more work to the parallel phase to reduce overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, idf_dict, terms_chunk)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (inverted_index_chunk, doc_vectors_chunk, doc_norms_chunk)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs, idf_dict, terms_chunk = args\n\n    inverted_index_chunk = {}\n    doc_vectors_chunk = {}\n    doc_norms_chunk = {}\n\n    # Build posting lists and doc vectors for each term in this chunk\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        if term not in idf_dict:\n            continue\n\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        if posting_list:\n            posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            inverted_index_chunk[term] = posting_list\n\n        # Update document vectors\n        for doc_id, tf in [(doc_id, tf_dict[term]) for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items() if term in tf_dict]:\n            if doc_id not in doc_vectors_chunk:\n                doc_vectors_chunk[doc_id] = {}\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vectors_chunk[doc_id][term] = tfidf\n\n    # Compute norms from accumulated vectors\n    for doc_id, doc_vector in doc_vectors_chunk.items():\n        norm_squared = sum(v * v for v in doc_vector.values())\n        doc_norms_chunk[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return inverted_index_chunk, doc_vectors_chunk, doc_norms_chunk\n\n\ndef _merge_partial_results(\n    partial_results: List[PartialIndexingResult],\n) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, float]], Dict[int, Set[str]], Set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Merge partial results from multiple workers.\n\n    Args:\n        partial_results: List of PartialIndexingResult from workers\n\n    Returns:\n        Merged (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    merged_doc_term_freqs: Dict[int, Dict[str,
